| stall\_exploration | enable/disable stall on exploration |  | exploration |  |
| start\_civil\_war | Make the rebels rise up against the country, starting a civil war. Has no effect if the country is already in a civil war. Will scope immediately into the rebel country tag. |  | rebels | country |
| start\_conquistador | Creates a new conquistador in the current country | ```  start_conquistador = {  area = <area>  location = <location in area to make capital>  character = <character>  price = <price>  price_modifier = <number> } ``` | country |  |
| start\_exploration | Creates a new exploration in the current country | ``` start_exploration = {  area = <area>  character = <character>  price = <price>  price_modifier = <number> } ``` | country |  |
| start\_mission | The country starts immediately the specified mission. Will not do anything if a mission is active already. | ``` start_mission = {  mission = <mission_scope>  [optional any parameters to pass in, eg target = x] } ``` | country |  |
| start\_periphora\_towards | Starts a periphora towards the target location. Art will move towards the destination slowly in a procession. |  | work\_of\_art | location |
| start\_revolt | Make the rebels rise up against the country in the form of a revolt. |  | rebels |  |
| start\_tutorial\_lesson | Starts the tutorial lesson with the given key. Does nothing if the tutorial is not running, the lesson is completed (or already running), or the lesson cannot be triggered (e.g. trigger fails) |  | none |  |
| start\_weather\_system | Starts off a new weather system. | ``` start_weather_system = {  width = <pixels>  length = <pixels>  strength = [0..1]  speed = <pixels_per_day>  type = <front/cyclone/tornado>  location = <start_location>  location = <waypoint>  [location = <waypoint>...] } ``` | none | weather\_system |
| start\_work\_of\_art | Makes the target character start working on some art. | ``` start_work_of_art = {  work_of_art_type = work_of_art_type:<type> (optional, will start a random type of WoA if not set)  quality = <calc> (optional, will take artist skill into consideration if unset)  show_quality = yes/no (by default no, shows the probable quality of the WoA before it is finished) } ``` | character |  |
| stop\_annexing\_country | scope country stops annexing the target country |  | country | country |
| stop\_cabinet\_action | Stop the cabinet doing stuff |  | cabinet |  |
| stop\_tutorial | Stops the tutorial. |  | none |  |
| support\_rebel | scope country starts supporting the target rebel |  | country | rebels |
| switch | Switch on a trigger for the evaluation of another trigger with an optional fallback trigger. | ``` switch = { 	trigger = simple_assign_trigger 	case_1 = { <effects> } 	case_2 = { <effects> } 	case_n = { <effects> } 	fallback = { <effects> } } ``` | none |  |
| take\_over\_all\_wars | takes over all wars where target country is warleader |  | country | country |
| test\_log | Log a string to the test log when this effect executes, test\_log = message, the message can be a localization string with ROOT, SCOPE and PREV available. | ``` test_log = {  name = <test_key>  text = <custom_log_message> } ``` | none |  |
| transfer\_location\_occupation | Transfers occupation of a Location to the target country |  | location | country |
| transfer\_subject | Transfers scope subject to target country's overlordship |  | country | country |
| transfer\_yearly\_gold | Transfers a proportion of your yearly gold to the target country |  | country |  |
| transfer\_yearly\_manpower | Transfers a proportion of your yearly Manpower to the target country |  | country |  |
| transfer\_yearly\_sailors | Transfers a proportion of your yearly sailors to the target country |  | country |  |
| trigger\_event\_non\_silently | triggers an event or on\_action, but shows the name of the event | ``` trigger_event_non_silently = {  id = X  days/months/years = Y } (for events) ``` | none |  |
| trigger\_event\_silently | triggers an event or on\_action | ``` trigger_event = {  id = X  days/months/years = Y } (for events) or trigger_event = {  on_action = X  days/months/years = Y } (for on_actions) Days/months/years are optional and equal to 0 if not specified. If specified, Y can be a value or an inclusive interval "{ A B }" from which the duration will be picked randomly. ``` | none |  |
| unlock\_unit | Unlocks a locked Unit |  | unit |  |
| unset\_participated\_in\_parliament | Remove the mark of the country as having participated in the parliament of the target international organization. |  | country | international\_organization |
| update\_leadership | Update the leadership panel of the target international organization. |  | none | international\_organization |
| while | Repeats enclosed effects while limit criteria are met or until set iteration count is reached | ``` while = {  limit = { <triggers> }  <effects> } while = {  count = 3  <effects> } Default max of 1000. ``` | none |  |
| white\_peace | forces a white peace in the target war |  | none | war |
